⚠️ Held-back article changed in Contentful and is included again – check the Anna-only answer:

  • How do I file a complaint? – Says complaints must be reported within 10 days. The complaint policy (Complaint Term 26) uses 14 days from delivery; the policy decides. Released automatically once the text no longer says 10 days.
  • What is the status of my complaint? – Swedish text is written for the new help centre (Mitt konto → ...), which the Swedish site does not have yet. Released automatically once the text no longer uses that path.
  • Reset your password or get back into your account – Swedish text is written for the new help centre (Mitt konto → ...), which the Swedish site does not have yet. Released automatically once the text no longer uses that path.
  • How long does a design check take? – Times differ from operations (about 70% autocheck in ~5 min, manual 30-60 min; Anna-only folder). Released automatically once the text is corrected.

  • What is Secured Delivery? – Says Secured Delivery does not cover loss. Correct: lost -> reprint/refund, late -> 20% refund (Anna-only folder). Released automatically once the text is corrected.

  • What happens if I miss my proof approval deadline?: Contact our Customer Service team, and they look at the possibilities with you.

  • How refunds work: Refund not arrived after 8 working days? Contact us with your order number and we'll trace it with the payment provider.
  • Why is my order "On hold"?: Unsure? Contact us with your order number.
  • Why have I not received my order confirmation?: Please contact us and we will happily assist you.
  • My payment has failed, what should I do?: If your payment fails a second time, please contact our print experts.
  • I can't find the product I'm looking for, what can I do?: You can reach out to us via chat or fill in a quote form, so our print experts can help you out with a bespoke order.
  • Adding elements and images: Still unsure? Check out Canva’s tutorials here, or get in touch with our customer service team who would be happy to help!
  • Can I change my delivery date?: Did you choose the wrong delivery date and do we offer a quicker one online? You can contact us and we can easily change your order and link the payments that were already made, as long as your order has not reached the production stage yet.
  • My track and trace code is not working, what can I do?: If by the following day, the link is still not active, please contact us and we can investigate the issue for you.
  • What is a digital proof?: Not sure what to change? Chat with our chat assistant Anna or contact our Customer Service team and we look at it with you.
  • Can I repeat a previous order?: If that does not work, you can contact us via our contact form.
  • How can I find my previous Canva designs?: If you're unable to locate a Canva design you’ve previously worked on but have already placed an order using that design, you can retrieve it by providing your order number to our customer service team.
  • How can I change the design I made in Canva once I accept it?: If your order has already been paid for, just get in touch with our printexperts  team  we can do our best to help!
  • How do I upload my own images in Canva?: Check out Canva’s tutorials here, or get in touch with our customer experience team who would be happy to help!
  • How do I pay on invoice?: Requesting different payment terms or credit limit  If you need a higher credit limit, or different payment terms, or want to enable the invoice payment option earlier, please contact our support team.
  • Why is my discount voucher not working? : If the code is not expired and is applicable to all products in your cart, please contact us via this form, and we will assist you with your issue.
  • Can I see a proof before paying for my order?: or contact us
  • I'm missing the accessory I ordered with my purchase. What now?: If you've thoroughly looked around and still can't locate your package, please reach out to us via this form.
  • I'm missing the accessory I ordered with my purchase. What now?: When you contact us, select the 'Complaint' and clearly mention the order, delivered items, and what's missing.
  • Can I delete my account?: If you'd still like to go ahead, contact us via our contact form and select the 'Other' category, letting us know you'd like your account deleted.
  • How do I unsubscribe from the newsletter?: If you've unchecked all topics but still receive emails from us, please don't hesitate to reach out to our print experts here.
  • I have a very big file, how can I upload this?: If you have any questions or need further assistance, please don't hesitate to reach out to our friendly support team.
  • I have overpaid my order, how can this be resolved?  : If it hasn't arrived by then, contact us and we'll help.
  • Why did the delivery date change since I placed the order?: If not, contact us, and we'll assist you further.

⚠️ Held-back article changed in Contentful and is included again – check the Anna-only answer:

  • Wat is de status van mijn klacht? – Swedish text is written for the new help centre (Mitt konto → ...), which the Swedish site does not have yet. Released automatically once the text no longer uses that path.
  • Wat is verzekerd verzenden? – Says Secured Delivery does not cover loss. Correct: lost -> reprint/refund, late -> 20% refund (Anna-only folder). Released automatically once the text is corrected.
  • Ik ben mijn wachtwoord vergeten. Wat moet ik doen? – Swedish text is written for the new help centre (Mitt konto → ...), which the Swedish site does not have yet. Released automatically once the text no longer uses that path.

Held back (known wrong text):

  • Hoe dien ik een klacht in? – Says complaints must be reported within 10 days. The complaint policy (Complaint Term 26) uses 14 days from delivery; the policy decides. Released automatically once the text no longer says 10 days.
  • Hoe lang duurt een bestands controle? – Times differ from operations (about 70% autocheck in ~5 min, manual 30-60 min; Anna-only folder). Released automatically once the text is corrected.
Sentences removed because they refer to customer service (19)
  • Waarom heb ik nog geen bestelbevestiging ontvangen? : Neem in dat geval contact met ons op.
  • De bestelling is niet compleet. Wat moet ik doen?: Als je ziet dat er geen extra pakket onderweg is, kun je contact met ons opnemen via het klachtenformulier.
  • Mijn betaling is mislukt. Wat moet ik doen?: Mocht je toch twijfelen of hulp nodig hebben, aarzel dan niet om contact met ons op te nemen.
  • Waarom heb ik een betalingsherinnering ontvangen?: Heb je jouw bestelling al betaald? Neem dan contact met ons op en stuur ons het betalingsbewijs.
  • Kan ik mijn bestelling retourneren?: Is je bestelling beschadigd aangekomen of is er een productiefout gemaakt? Neem dan direct contact met ons op.
  • Kan ik mijn bestelling retourneren?: Weet je niet zeker of jouw product gepersonaliseerd is of in aanmerking komt voor retour? Neem gerust contact met ons op, we helpen je graag verder!
  • Kan ik mijn leverdatum aanpassen?: Heb je de verkeerde leverdatum gekozen en bieden we online een snellere leverdatum aan? Zolang je order nog niet in productie is kan je contact met ons opnemen en kunnen we eenvoudig de order aanpassen.
  • Mijn Track & Trace code doet het niet. Wat kan ik doen? : Als de link de volgende dag nog steeds niet actief is, neem dan contact met ons op  zodat we voor je kunnen onderzoeken waar het probleem zit.
  • Ik heb mijn bestelling geannuleerd. Waarom heb ik nog geen terugbetaling ontvangen?: Indien geen van bovenstaande van toepassing is zouden we je willen vragen om contact met ons op te nemen.
  • Ik heb mijn factuur niet ontvangen. Wat moet ik doen?: Kan je de gewenste factuur nog steeds niet vinden of wil je de factuur al eerder ontvangen? Neem dan contact met onze klantenservice op.
  • Kan ik een vorige bestelling herhalen? : Als dat niet werkt, kun je contact met ons opnemen via ons contactformulier.
  • Hoe upload ik mijn eigen afbeeldingen in Canva?: Weet je het nog steeds niet zeker? Bekijk Canva's tutorials hier of neem contact op met onze klantenservice, zij helpen je graag!
  • Hoe kan ik op rekening betalen?: Als je een hogere kredietlimiet of andere betalingsvoorwaarden nodig hebt, of als je de optie voor betaling per factuur eerder wilt inschakelen, neem dan contact op met onze klantenservice.
  • Waarom werkt mijn kortingsvoucher niet?: Als je zeker weet dat de code niet verlopen is en de code van toepassing is op de bestelde producten, kun je contact met ons opnemen via dit formulier.
  • Ik mis de asseccoire die ik heb besteld bij mijn bestelling. Wat nu?: Als je grondig hebt gezocht en nog steeds je pakket niet kunt vinden, neem dan contact met ons op via dit formulier.
  • Ik mis de asseccoire die ik heb besteld bij mijn bestelling. Wat nu?: Wanneer je contact met ons opneemt, selecteer dan 'Klacht' en vermeld duidelijk de bestelling, de geleverde items en wat er ontbreekt.
  • Ik heb te veel betaald voor mijn bestelling, hoe wordt dit opgelost? : Als je het geld dan nog niet hebt ontvangen, kun je contact met ons opnemen voor verdere begeleiding.
  • Wat betekent de status van mijn bestelling?: Je kunt ook zelf contact met ons opnemen voor meer informatie.
  • Waarom is de leverdatum veranderd sinds ik de bestelling heb geplaatst?: Zo niet, neem dan contact met ons op en we helpen u verder.
Left out because a policy covers them (11)
  • Kan ik mijn bestelling annuleren? → Cancel Order 27
  • Kan ik mijn bestelling aanpassen? → Artwork Adjustments 26 / Upgrade Downgrade 26
  • Kan ik mijn afleveradres wijzigen? → Change Delivery Address 26
  • Kunnen wij een ontwerp voor jou maken? → Decided: no design help at all. The website says small changes are possible (wrong). Kept out until the text is fixed; the Tribal folders keep the correct answer.
  • Hoe neem ik contact op? → Contact Agent (Anna is support)
  • Hoe kan ik het Canva ontwerp veranderen nadat ik het geaccepteerd heb? → Legacy shops: the website says 'editable until paid', which is the new-stack (IE/IT) rule. On legacy shops a Canva-plugin design can be edited until production starts (Tribal folder). US: no Canva.

  • Warum habe ich meine Bestellbestätigung nicht erhalten?: Bitte nehmen Sie Kontakt mit uns und wir helfen Ihnen gerne weiter.

  • Kann ich mein Lieferdatum ändern?: Haben Sie den falschen Liefertermin gewählt und bieten wir online einen schnelleren an? Nehmen Sie Kontakt mit uns auf und wir können Ihre Bestellung problemlos ändern und die bereits geleisteten Zahlungen verknüpfen, sofern Ihre Bestellung noch nicht in die Produktionsphase gelangt ist.
  • Mein Track & Trace-Code funktioniert nicht, was kann ich tun?: Wenn der Link am nächsten Tag immer noch nicht aktiv ist, nehmen Sie bitte Kontakt mit uns auf.

  • Warum funktioniert mein Rabattgutschein nicht?: Wenn der Code noch nicht abgelaufen ist und für alle Produkte in Ihrem Warenkorb gilt, nehmen Sie bitte Kontakt mit uns auf über dieses Formular.
  • Mir fehlt das Zubehör, das ich beim Kauf bestellt habe. Was jetzt?: Wenn Sie sich gründlich umgesehen haben und Ihr Paket immer noch nicht finden können, nehmen Sie bitte Kontakt mit uns auf über dieses Formular.

Sentences removed because they refer to customer service (28)
  • Why have I not received my order confirmation?: Please contact us and we will happily assist you.
  • What is the status of my complaint?: While it's currently not possible to track the status of your complaint, you can always contact us for updates.
  • My payment has failed, what should I do?: If your payment fails a second time, please contact our print experts.
  • Why did I receive a payment reminder?: Have you already paid for the order? Please contact us and send us your proof of payment for your payment.
  • Can I return my order?: Of course, if your order arrives damaged or there is a production issue, please contact us.
  • Can I return my order?: If you’re unsure whether your product is personalised or eligible for return, feel free to contact us.
  • I can't find the product I'm looking for, what can I do?: You can reach out to us via chat or fill in a quote form, so our print experts can help you out with a bespoke order.
  • Adding elements and images: Still unsure? Check out Canva’s tutorials here, or get in touch with our customer service team who would be happy to help!
  • Can I change my delivery date?: Did you choose the wrong delivery date and do we offer a quicker one online? You can contact us and we can easily change your order and link the payments that were already made, as long as your order has not reached the production stage yet.
  • My track and trace code is not working, what can I do?: If by the following day, the link is still not active, please contact us and we can investigate the issue for you.
  • I have cancelled my order, why have I not received my refund?: If neither of these reasons apply, please contact us so we can investigate the issue further.
  • I have not received my invoice, now what?: If you can't see the correct invoice or you want to receive it earlier, you can contact us so we can send it to you.
  • Can I repeat a previous order?: If that does not work, you can contact us via our contact form.
  • How can I download my design as PDF in Canva?: Once you’ve placed your order, you can contact our customer service team directly, who can download your design and send it to you - just let us know your order number!
  • How can I find my previous Canva designs?: If you're unable to locate a Canva design you’ve previously worked on but have already placed an order using that design, you can retrieve it by providing your order number to our customer service team.
  • How do I upload my own images in Canva?: Check out Canva’s tutorials here, or get in touch with our customer experience team who would be happy to help!
  • How do I pay on invoice?: If you need a higher credit limit, or different payment terms, or want to enable the invoice payment option earlier, please contact our support team.
  • Why is my discount voucher not working? : If the code is not expired and is applicable to all products in your cart, please contact us via this form, and we will assist you with your issue.
  • Can I see a proof before paying for my order?: or contact us
  • I'm missing the accessory I ordered with my purchase. What now?: If you've thoroughly looked around and still can't locate your package, please reach out to us via this form.
  • I'm missing the accessory I ordered with my purchase. What now?: When you contact us, select the 'Complaint' and clearly mention the order, delivered items, and what's missing.
  • How do I increase my credit limit?: Contact Our Team: Contact our Customer Experience here.
  • Can I delete my account?: To proceed, please contact us via our contact form and select the 'other' category.
  • How do I unsubscribe from the newsletter?: If you've unchecked all topics but still receive emails from us, please don't hesitate to reach out to our print experts here.
  • I have a very big file, how can I upload this?: If you have any questions or need further assistance, please don't hesitate to reach out to our friendly support team.
  • I have overpaid my order, how can this be resolved? : If you have not received the funds then, you can contact us for further guidance.
  • What does the status assigned to my order mean?: You may also reach out to us for updates.
  • Why did the delivery date change since I placed the order?: If not, contact us, and we'll assist you further.
